============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3&                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n                canSplit = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!canSplit) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : center.z;\n\n                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\n                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\n                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n            }\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {\n        printf(\"Sample[%zu]: %.17e\\n\", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));\n    }\n    printf(\"Hash: %016\" PRIx64 \"\\n\", h.get());\n    printf(\"=== END Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return Need exact same rounding. raw -> float conversion rounds raw to nearest representable float (24-bit), then multiply by 2^-32 exactly (power of two, no rounding beyond exponent shift). The CPU computation is `raw / 4294967296.0` in double then cast to float? For generate_canonical, implementation computes using long double? Let's check libstdc++ generate_canonical. For float, it uses `__ret = ... / __tmp` where __tmp = R^k as float? Let's look at the header to be exact. But empirically `(float)((double)raw * 2^-32)` matches. On GPU, convert raw to float with round-to-nearest then scale by 2^-32: (float)raw loses low bits with round-to-nearest; multiply by exact power of two. That should equal (float)((double)raw * 2^-32) because double representation exact, multiply exact power of 2, then rounding to float of value = raw*2^-32; rounding raw to 24 bits after shifting? Scaling by power of 2 doesn't change significand, so rounding the product is same as rounding raw to 24-bit at corresponding exponent. Should match __uint2float_rn(raw) * 2^-32. Let's verify on device later maybe.\n\nNow MT19937 on GPU. Since all random values come from single global sequence and we process pairs in a fixed order, we need per-pair access. Options:\n\nA) Precompute the entire random value array on CPU (or GPU) then kernel reads. For N=320 and 1280 memory okay; N=5120 6.7GB maybe fits in 24GB but CPU time. But we can process in chunks. This is simplest and exact. To improve performance, generate on CPU using std::mt19937 and feed. But maybe we can generate on GPU much faster while still exact using MT with jump-ahead. However complexity/risk. Let's evaluate an alternate: Do we actually need to precompute random values for all pairs? Each pair uses exactly 64 draws, so we can store 64 floats per pair = 256 bytes per pair. For chunk of 65536 pairs = 16MB. Good.\n\nB) Precompute per-pair mt19937 *state* on CPU, upload states, generate floats on GPU. More data (2.5KB/pair) and CPU extra work; worse than just generating floats on CPU. So A is better.\n\nC) Implement CPU MT jump for chunk starts and generate floats on GPU in parallel: For each chunk of, say, 4096 pairs, we need 262144 consecutive mt outputs. We can generate these exactly on GPU by starting one mt state per chunk (computed on CPU via jump or sequential run) and having threads cooperate? MT sequential by definition; one thread per chunk could generate 262k values, which is slow per thread but number of chunks in parallel maybe many. For N=320, 100k pairs / 4096 = 25 chunks, only 25 threads -> terrible. For N=5120, 6400 chunks -> 6400 threads across ~120 SMs (3090 has 82 SMs, 4 blocks each) -> decent maybe. But MT generation on GPU single thread per chunk ~ maybe 10-30M outputs/s; 6400 threads * 20M = 128G outputs/s? No, per-thread throughput limited by instruction issue; each SM can run many warps. With one thread per chunk and 25 chunks, only 25 SM threads used; but for large N many chunks. Still chunk generation would be similar total work as CPU but on GPU, with parallelism across chunks. Could be fast for large N. But jump-ahead for chunk start needed. We can compute chunk-start states on CPU sequentially once per chunk (advance by 64*C per chunk via generating/discarding? expensive) or via jump. Alternatively, we don't need exact MT state for chunk start if we generate on CPU for first chunk... no.\n\nSimpler: Precompute floats on CPU in chunks. Let's assess whether this truly hurts performance. We can run CPU RNG generation in a separate std::thread overlapping GPU kernel execution (double buffering). That hides CPU time if GPU compute is longer. For default small case GPU kernel may finish quickly and CPU becomes bottleneck, but total still maybe much faster than 1.5s? Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n         newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm) - device\n// ============================================================================\n\nHOST_DEVICE val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                                       const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return 3.402823466e+38F;\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 3.402823466e+38F;\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 3.402823466e+38F;\n\n    return                 const GpuOctNode* __restrict__ nodes,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ leafTriangles,\n                                   const Triangle* __restrict__              const GpuOctNode& child = nodes[childIdx];\n                if (rayIntersectsBoxDevice(from, to, child.center, child.halfExtent)) {\n                    if (sp < 32) stack[sp++] = - device\n// ============================================================================\n\nHOST_DEVICE val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n__device__ val_t computeKijDevice(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                                  const Triangle*      const val_t* __restrict__ randomValues,\n                                  int randomOffset) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n    const val_t* rnd = randomValues + randomOffset;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        // Random point in triI\n        val_t u = rnd[0];\n        val_t v =  const Vec3 pJ = triJ.a + (triJ.b - triJ.a) * u + (triJ.c - triJ.a) * v;\n\n        if (isRayBlockedDevice(pI, pJ, nodes, leafTriangles, triangles,\n                               static_cast<int>(idxI), static_cast<int>(idxJ))) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        const Vec3 vv = pJ - pI;\n        const val_t distSqr = vv.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        const val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(vv, triI.normal());\n        const val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-vv, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n__global__ void computeKijChunkKernel(size_t pairStart,\n                                      int pairCount,\n                                      const val_t* __restrict__ randomValues,\n                                      const int* __restrict__ randomOffsets,\n                                      const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,\n                                      size_t numTriangles,\n                                      const GpuOctNode* __restrict__ nodes,\n                                      const int* __restrict__ leafTriangles,\n                                      val_t* __restrict__ kij) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= pairCount) return;\n\n    const int offset = randomOffsets[local];\n    const size_t pair = pairStart + static_cast<size_t>(local);\n\n    const size_t rowSize = numTriangles - 1;\n    const size_t i = pair / rowSize;\n    const size_t rem = pair - i * rowSize;\n    const size_t j = (rem < i) ? rem : rem + 1;\n\n    if (offset < 0) {\n        // Triangle pair is culled by the same normal test used by the\n        // sequential implementation (and consumes no random numbers).\n        kij[i * numTriangles + j] = ZERO;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    kij[i * numTriangles + j] = computeKijDevice(\n        i, j, triangles, nodes, leafTriangles, randomValues, offset);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation - device\n// ============================================================================\n\n__global__ void computeTauKernel(size_t numTriangles,\n                                 const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,\n                                 int* __restrict__ tau) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= numTriangles || j >= numTriangles) return;\n\n    if (i == j) {\n        tau[i * numTriangles + j] = 0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const Vec3 ci = triangles[i].center();\n    const Vec3 cj = triangles[j].center();\n    const val_t dist = (ci - cj).norm();\n    tau[i * numTriangles + j] = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    // GPU resources\n    Triangle* d_triangles = nullptr;\n    GpuOctNode* d_nodes = nullptr;\n    int* d_leafTriangles = nullptr;\n    int d_numNodes = 0;\n    val_t* d_areas = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_rho = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_kij = nullptr;\n    int* d_tau = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_radE = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_radB = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_distances = nullptr;\n\n    SimulationState() : numTriangles(0), numTimesteps(0), sourceIndex(0) {}\n\n    ~SimulationState() { freeDevice(); }\n\n    SimulationState(const SimulationState&) = delete;\n    SimulationState& operator=(const SimulationState&) = delete;\n\n    void freeDevice() {\n        if (d_triangles) cudaFree(d_triangles);\n        if (d_nodes) cudaFree(d_nodes);\n        if (d_leafTriangles) cudaFree(d_leafTriangles);\n        if (d_areas) cudaFree(d_areas);\n        if (d_rho) cudaFree(d_rho);\n        if (d_kij) cudaFree(d_kij);\n        if (d_tau) cudaFree(d_tau);\n        if (d_radE) cudaFree(d_radE);\n        if (d_radB) cudaFree(d_radB);\n        if (d_distances) cudaFree(d_distances);\n        d_triangles = nullptr;\n        d_nodes = nullptr;\n        d_leafTriangles = nullptr;\n        d_areas = nullptr;\n        d_rho = nullptr;\n        d_kij = nullptr;\n        d_tau = nullptr;\n        d_radE = nullptr;\n        d_radB = nullptr;\n        d_distances = nullptr;\n    }\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n\n    // Flatten octree for the GPU and upload all immutable data.\n    printf(\"Uploading simulation data to GPU...\\n\");\n    std::vector<GpuOctNode> flatNodes;\n    std::vector<int> flatLeafTriangles;\n    flattenOctreeNode(state.octree, flatNodes, flatLeafTriangles);\n    state.d_numNodes = static_cast<int>(flatNodes.size());\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t tn = timesteps * n;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_triangles, n * sizeof(Triangle)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_nodes, flatNodes.size() * sizeof(GpuOctNode)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_leafTriangles, flatLeafTriangles.size() * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_areas, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_rho, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_kij, n * n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_tau, n * n * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_radE, tn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_radB, tn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&state.d_distances, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_triangles, state.triangles.data(),\n                          n * sizeof(Triangle), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_nodes, flatNodes.data(),\n                          flatNodes.size() * sizeof(GpuOctNode), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_leafTriangles, flatLeafTriangles.data(),\n                          flatLeafTriangles.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_areas, state.areas.data(),\n                          n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_rho, state.rho.data(),\n                          n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.d_radE, state.radE.data(),\n                          tn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(state.d_kij, 0, n * n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(state.d_radB, 0, tn * sizeof(val_t)));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    if (n < 2) return;\n\n    const size_t totalPairs = n * (n - 1);\n    const size_t rowSize = n - 1;\n\n    // The reference implementation uses one mt19937 stream seeded with 42 and\n    // consumes 64 values for every non-culled triangle pair, in row-major\n    // order.  Reproduce that stream exactly on the host, then stream the\n    // samples to the device in chunks.\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    struct ChunkBuffer {\n        val_t* h_random = nullptr;\n        int* h_offsets = nullptr;\n        val_t* d_random = nullptr;\n        int* d_offsets = nullptr;\n        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n        cudaEvent_t copyDone = nullptr;\n        bool used = false;\n    };\n\n    ChunkBuffer buffers[2];\n    const size_t randomCapacity = KIJ_CHUNK_PAIRS * static_cast<size_t>(NUM_RAYS * 4);\n\n    for (ChunkBuffer& buf : buffers) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&buf.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&buf.copyDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buf.h_random),\n                                 randomCapacity * sizeof(val_t), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&buf.h_offsets),\n                                 KIJ_CHUNK_PAIRS * sizeof(int), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&buf.d_random, randomCapacity * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&buf.d_offsets, KIJ_CHUNK_PAIRS * sizeof(int)));\n    }\n\n    size_t lastReportedTriangle = 0;\n    int bufferIndex = 0;\n\n    for (size_t pairStart = 0; pairStart < totalPairs; pairStart += KIJ_CHUNK_PAIRS) {\n        ChunkBuffer& buf = buffers[bufferIndex];\n\n        // Make sure the host staging buffer is no longer being copied.\n        if (buf.used) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(buf.copyDone));\n        }\n\n        const size_t remaining = totalPairs - pairStart;\n        const int pairCount = static_cast<int>(\n            std::min<size_t>(KIJ_CHUNK_PAIRS, remaining));\n\n        size_t randomCount = 0;\n        for (int local = 0; local < pairCount; ++local) {\n            const size_t pair = pairStart + static_cast<size_t>(local);\n            const size_t i = pair / rowSize;\n            const size_t rem = pair - i * rowSize;\n            const size_t j = (rem < i) ? rem : rem + 1;\n\n            if (state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f) {\n                buf.h_offsets[local] = -1;\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            buf.h_offsets[local] = static_cast<int>(randomCount);\n            for (int s = 0; s < NUM_RAYS * 4; ++s) {\n                buf.h_random[randomCount++] = rng.rand();\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (randomCount > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(buf.d_random, buf.h_random,\n                                       randomCount * sizeof(val_t),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, buf.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(buf.d_offsets, buf.h_offsets,\n                                   pairCount * sizeof(int),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, buf.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(buf.copyDone, buf.stream));\n        buf.used = true;\n\n        const int threads = 256;\n        const int blocks = (pairCount + threads - 1) / threads;\n        computeKijChunkKernel<<<blocks, threads, 0, buf.stream>>>(\n            pairStart, pairCount, buf.d_random, buf.d_offsets, state.d_triangles,\n            n, state.d_nodes, state.d_leafTriangles, state.d_kij);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        const size_t lastPair = pairStart + static_cast<size_t>(pairCount);\n        size_t lastTriangle = lastPair / rowSize;\n        if (lastPair == totalPairs) lastTriangle = n;\n        if (lastTriangle >= lastReportedTriangle + 100 || lastTriangle == n) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\",\n                   std::min(lastTriangle, n), n);\n            lastReportedTriangle = lastTriangle;\n        }\n\n        bufferIndex ^= 1;\n    }\n\n    for (ChunkBuffer& buf : buffers) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(buf.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(buf.copyDone));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(buf.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(buf.h_random));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(buf.h_offsets));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(buf.d_random));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(buf.d_offsets));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    if (n == 0) return;\n\n    const dim3 block(16, 16);\n    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                    static_cast<unsigned>((n + block.y - 1) / block.y));\n    computeTauKernel<<<grid, block>>>(n, state.d_triangles, state.d_tau);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\n__global__ void simulateStepKernel(int t,\n                                   size_t numTriangles,\n                                   const val_t* __restrict__ kij,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ tau,\n                                   const val_t* __restrict__ areas,\n                                   const val_t* __restrict__ rho,\n                                   const val_t* __restrict__ radE,\n                                   val_t* __restrict__ radB) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= numTriangles) return;\n\n    const size_t rowOffset = i * numTriangles;\n    val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numTriangles; ++j) {\n        if (i == j) continue;\n\n        const int tauij = tau[rowOffset + j];\n\n        // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n        if (t < tauij) continue;\n\n        const val_t kijVal = kij[rowOffset + j];\n        if (kijVal <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n        const size_t srcTime = static_cast<size_t>(t) - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n        const val_t radJ = radB[srcTime * numTriangles + j];\n        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n        sumB += std::min(kijVal * areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n    }\n\n    // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(t) * numTriangles + i;\n    radB[offset] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[offset];\n}\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t t = state.numTimesteps;\n    const int threads = 256;\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((n + threads - 1) / threads);\n\n    for (size_t step = 0; step < t; ++step) {\n        simulateStepKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(\n            static_cast<int>(step), n, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.d_areas,\n            state.d_rho, state.d_radE, state.d_radB);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        if ((step + 1) % 10 == 0 || step + 1 == t) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", step + 1, t);\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\n__global__ void computeDistancesKernel(size_t numTriangles,\n                                       size_t numTimesteps,\n                                       size_t sourceIndex,\n                                       const val_t* __restrict__ radB,\n                                       val_t* __restrict__ distances) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= numTriangles) return;\n\n    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n    int bestT = 0;\n\n    // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n        for (size_t tt = t; tt < numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n            const val_t pB = radB[tt * numTriangles + i];\n            const val_t pS = radB[(tt - t) * numTriangles + sourceIndex];\n            sum += pS * pB;\n        }\n\n        if (sum > maxCorr) {\n            maxCorr = sum;\n            bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n        }\n    }\n\n    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n}\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int threads = 256;\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((n + threads - 1) / threads);\n\n    computeDistancesKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(\n        n, state.numTimesteps, state.sourceIndex, state.d_radB, state.d_distances);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(), state.d_distances,\n                          n * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-o\") == 0) {\n            printResults = Maybe there's a much simpler way: Since we need 64 consecutive outputs starting at index 64p, and MT19937 has a tempering function applied to state words. The output sequence is generated by twisting: outputs are tempered state[i] for i=0..623, then regenerate. To get output at arbitrary index k, we need state at that point. There is a known cache-friendly algorithm: store states at block boundaries computed by a fast jump. The jump can be done using the `jump` function that uses polynomial powers; cost O(19937/64 * bits) maybe ~10k word ops. 6.5M/64 pairs? Wait per pair one jump -> e.g., N=320 102k jumps * 10k = 1B ops, ~0.5s, worse than current 24ms. Unless we jump per group of pairs (e.g., one warp = 32 pairs), then only 3200 jumps, and each thread advances within group up to 32*64=2048 steps. That's 2048 MT steps per pair? No, per warp one state; each lane needs its pair's 64 values; could have lane 0 advance state and broadcast outputs? Complex.\n\nAlternative: GPU mt19937 generation with one state per block (block processes a contiguous run of pairs). Each block maintains its own MT state initialized at the run start (computed via jump on host once per block). Inside block, one thread (or warp) generates the 64 random values per pair into shared memory, then all threads process? But block would process pairs sequentially, limiting parallelism within block. Number of blocks can be large, each handling a run. E.g., block=256 threads, each block handles a run of 4096 pairs: thread 0 generates 4096*64=262k values sequentially (~ms at 100M/s = 2.6ms) writing to global, while other threads idle; then block computes 4096 pairs with 256 threads. Pipeline double buffering within block could overlap RNG generation (one warp) with compute (other warps).
